--
-- Table structure for table `MAINTENANCE_EVENT_HISTORY`
--

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `MAINTENANCE_EVENT_HISTORY` (
  `ID` int(11) NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `RECORD_ID` int(11) DEFAULT NULL,
  `TIMESTAMP` datetime(6) DEFAULT NULL,
  `USER` varchar(255) DEFAULT NULL,
  `EVENT_TYPE_ID` int(11) DEFAULT NULL,
  `AUTOMATION_TYPE_ID` int(11) DEFAULT NULL,
  `COMMENT` varchar(4000) DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`),
  KEY `MAINTENANCE_EVENT_HISTORY_RECORDID_FK` (`RECORD_ID`),
  KEY `MAINTENANCE_EVENT_HISTORY_EVENTTYPEID_FK` (`EVENT_TYPE_ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=2000 DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb3 COLLATE=utf8mb3_general_ci;
